([lizhuoq/atmospheric-science-research-skills](https://github.com/lizhuoq/atmospheric-science-research-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/atmospheric-composition-air-quality) · ★1)
- **atmospheric-data-qc**: Design or audit quality control and harmonization for atmospheric observations, reanalyses, retrievals, and model output. Use for units, coordinates, calendars, timestamps, quality flags, missingness, aggregation, regridding, representativeness, provenance, and uncertainty before scientific analysis. ([lizhuoq/atmospheric-science-research-skills](https://github.com/lizhuoq/atmospheric-science-research-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/atmospheric-data-qc) · ★1)
- **atmospheric-extremes-attribution**: Design or audit analyses of atmospheric and hydroclimatic extremes and event attribution. Use for heatwaves, heavy precipitation, drought, storms, compound events, return periods, risk ratios, factual/counterfactual ensembles, and attribution claims; distinguish event description, trend, mechanism, and causation. ([lizhuoq/atmospheric-science-research-skills](https://github.com/lizhuoq/atmospheric-science-research-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/atmospheric-extremes-attribution) · ★1)
- **atmospheric-figure-analysis**: Interpret or audit atmospheric-science figures, tables, maps, profiles, and equations using visual inspection plus source context. Use for scientific meaning, uncertainty, panel consistency, graphical integrity, caption adequacy, and claim support; do not infer unreadable or unstated values. ([lizhuoq/atmospheric-science-research-skills](https://github.com/lizhuoq/atmospheric-science-research-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/atmospheric-figure-analysis) · ★1)
- **atmospheric-gap-imputation-eval**: Evaluates the ability of machine learning models to reconstruct missing multivariate atmospheric data across time and altitude. It probes spatiotemporal continuity, physical gradient preservation, and performance under varying gap lengths (short, medium, long). Use when the user wants to benchmark on SD-WACCM-X synthetic atmospheric data, or asks about evaluating this task. Reports Pearson correlation R. ([qhjqhj00/research-skills-pool](https://github.com/qhjqhj00/research-skills-pool/tree/HEAD/skill-factory/output/atmospheric-gap-imputation-eval) · ★3)

- **atmospheric-literature-search**: Design and execute reproducible searches for atmospheric-science literature, including source routing, query construction, citation chaining, deduplication, version checks, screening, and lawful full-text acquisition. Use when the task is to find or update a literature set; hand verified papers to a reading or synthesis workflow. ([lizhuoq/atmospheric-science-research-skills](https://github.com/lizhuoq/atmospheric-science-research-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/atmospheric-literature-search) · ★1)
- **atmospheric-manuscript-writer**: Draft or revise atmospheric-science manuscripts from verified literature evidence and user-owned methods and results. Use for titles, abstracts, introductions, methods, results narration, discussions, conclusions, cover letters, and revisions; never invent data, analyses, findings, novelty, or citations. ([lizhuoq/atmospheric-science-research-skills](https://github.com/lizhuoq/atmospheric-science-research-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/atmospheric-manuscript-writer) · ★1)
- **atmospheric-model-experiment**: Design or audit atmospheric numerical-model experiments, including NWP, WRF, chemistry transport, climate, LES, and Earth-system simulations. Use for domains, resolution, initialization, forcing, boundary conditions, spin-up, parameterizations, ensembles, sensitivity experiments, conservation, and reproducibility. ([lizhuoq/atmospheric-science-research-skills](https://github.com/lizhuoq/atmospheric-science-research-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/atmospheric-model-experiment) · ★1)
- **atmospheric-paper-reader**: Read atmospheric-science papers deeply and extract atomic, source-located evidence from text, methods, equations, tables, and figures. Use after papers have been supplied or lawfully acquired; do not use metadata or abstracts as substitutes for full-text evidence. ([lizhuoq/atmospheric-science-research-skills](https://github.com/lizhuoq/atmospheric-science-research-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/atmospheric-paper-reader) · ★1)
- **atmospheric-peer-review**: Conduct evidence-centered peer review of atmospheric-science manuscripts, proposals, and revisions. ([lizhuoq/atmospheric-science-research-skills](https://github.com/lizhuoq/atmospheric-science-research-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/atmospheric-peer-review) · ★1)

Covers pre-code audit, tested scroll-speed presets (0.08..0.60), 2.5D Sprite3D depth cards, patchy outline cleanup, Godot 4 / Unity shaders, and 6-point automated verification (`tools/atmospheric_perspective.py`). ([mulosbron/game-dev-skills](https://github.com/mulosbron/game-dev-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/atmospheric-perspective))
- **atmospheric-reentry-thermal**: TPS, hypersonic aerothermodynamics, reentry trajectories, and ablation ([LoopyLuci/Skills](https://github.com/LoopyLuci/Skills/tree/HEAD/skills/atmospheric-reentry-thermal) · ★2)
- **atmospheric-research-design**: Convert an atmospheric-science question and verified evidence into a feasible observational, modeling, statistical, or mixed research design.
